{"parentUuid":"e90ff083-0748-49a3-8c4a-3a61738627a8","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"aa8a9b2b-9af9-4d7c-83f7-7526b28d1edc","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_014rvL8bhgpSDX5uvkmmvTZj","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t---\n2\tstatus: CHARTER\n3\tratified_at: 2026-05-01\n4\tratified_by: Project owner — Q&A 2026-05-01-001 Q3=A explicit pick (S38 FOCUSED_IMPL Bundle 1 charter promote)\n5\tratifying_decision: D-017\n6\tcompanion_hook: scripts/hooks/tier1-bloat-check.sh (authored at S38 per Q3=A)\n7\tsource_evidence:\n8\t  - observations/queued-grill-master.md § Q-D3 (codify Tier 1/2/3 memory tiers; closed S15 Batch 1 — Yes, add memory-tiers.md to constitution)\n9\t  - session-plans/pending/003-S15-track-7-constitution-amendments.md § 4.2\n10\t  - CLAUDE.md § Session Protocol (existing implicit tiering)\n11\t  - agent-workspace/CLAUDE.md § Reading Priority for Agent (existing implicit tiering)\n12\t  - human-workspace/q-and-a/pending/2026-05-01-001-S35-charter-promote-batch.md § Q3=A (explicit pick — promote + author tier1-bloat-check.sh)\n13\tpredecessor_proposal: agent-workspace/proposals/memory-tiers.md (S16 draft; moved to constitution at S38)\n14\t---\n15\t\n16\t# Memory Tiers — CHARTER\n17\t\n18\t> **Status**: CHARTER (ratified 2026-05-01 at S38 via Q&A 2026-05-01-001 Q3=A). Edits require explicit user prompt + Q&A per `agent-workspace/CLAUDE.md` constitution-amendment process.\n19\t\n20\t## Purpose\n21\t\n22\tMake explicit the three tiers of context the agent draws from across a session. Without this codification, every agent re-derives the priority order from scattered prose and occasionally over-loads at SessionStart (blowing bootstrap budgets per `autonomous-protocol.md` Rule 4).\n23\t\n24\t## Tier Definitions\n25\t\n26\t### Tier 1 — Immutable Always-Loaded (bootstrap)\n27\t\n28\t**Loaded automatically at SessionStart by the harness** (CLAUDE.md hard rule + SessionStart hook injects). Counts against bootstrap ceiling per session type.\n29\t\n30\t| File | Why Tier 1 |\n31\t|---|---|\n32\t| `CLAUDE.md` | Project-wide identity + hard rules; needed every turn |\n33\t| `agent-workspace/CLAUDE.md` | Workspace contract; agent's domain rules |\n34\t| `agent-workspace/memory/current-execution.md` | THE single routing source of truth |\n35\t| `agent-workspace/memory/checkpoints/latest.md` (if recent ≤24h) | Resume context |\n36\t\n37\t**Total target size**: ≤8K tokens (per `autonomous-protocol.md` Rule 4 PLAN session ceiling). Tier 1 must stay tight; if it exceeds 8K consistently, files must be re-shaped or split (heaviest file becomes Tier 2 with explicit-load discipline).\n38\t\n39\t### Tier 2 — Just-In-Time (loaded on demand at first need)\n40\t\n41\t**Read when the active task surfaces them as needed**. Agent reads via Read tool; not auto-injected.\n42\t\n43\t| File | When to load |\n44\t|---|---|\n45\t| `PROJECT_CHARTER.md` | When charter principle being applied or charter clause being amended |\n46\t| `AGENT_OPERATING_MANUAL.md` | When dispatching subagent / picking session type / consulting model-routing rules |\n47\t| `agent-workspace/memory/project.md` | Phase-boundary review; consulting recent ADRs |\n48\t| `agent-workspace/memory/agent-notes.md` | Pattern recall; learning rule lookup |\n49\t| `agent-workspace/memory/mistake-log.md` | Pre-flight failure catalog (per agent-workspace/CLAUDE.md priority 5) |\n50\t| Last 3 files in `agent-workspace/memory/sessions/` | Recent session context |\n51\t| `agent-workspace/session-plans/pending/<active-plan>.md` | When executing a plan's specific track |\n52\t| `agent-workspace/constitution/<topic>.md` (the SPECIFIC one task touches) | Topical lookup |\n53\t| Specific spec in `specs/**/*.md` | When implementing the spec or verifying alignment |\n54\t| Specific skill `.claude/skills/<name>/SKILL.md` (beyond auto-discover) | When deep-diving into a procedure |\n55\t\n56\t### Tier 3 — Explicit Pull (loaded only when user or audit demands)\n57\t\n58\t**Never auto-loaded; read only on direct request or specific audit dispatch**.\n59\t\n60\t| File / directory | When to load |\n61\t|---|---|\n62\t| `agent-workspace/memory/sessions/` (older than last 3) | Post-mortem / archaeology |\n63\t| `agent-workspace/memory/decisions/<NNN>-*.md` (specific historical decision) | Cross-reference for new decision |\n64\t| `agent-workspace/memory/observations/` (subagent return artifacts) | When ingesting a specific subagent's output |\n65\t| `agent-workspace/memory/drift-logs/` | Drift audit dispatch |\n66\t| `agent-workspace/memory/post-mortems/` | Failure-driven learning |\n67\t| `agent-workspace/learning-data/index/` (RAG queryable) | Research scanner / promote-rule subagent |\n68\t| `agent-workspace/memory/thesis-log/` | THESIS sessions only (read-only on code per CLAUDE.md) |\n69\t| `obsidian-vault/wiki/<entity>.md` | Entity / concept lookup during INGEST or THESIS |\n70\t| `eval-sets/baseline-results/` | Eval regression review only |\n71\t\n72\t**Hard exclusion (never read in runtime path)**: `agent-workspace/learning-data/{events,archive}/` — write-only NDJSON queue per D-005 5.5d.1 + drift signal D9. Settings.json `permissions.deny` enforces.\n73\t\n74\t## Boundary Rules\n75\t\n76\t1. **Tier 1 never exceeds bootstrap ceiling per session type** (per `autonomous-protocol.md` Rule 4). If it would, redesign — split a file, pull non-essential prose to Tier 2.\n77\t2. **Tier 3 stays Tier 3**: don't auto-promote to Tier 2 just because it was useful once. Promotion requires phase-boundary review.\n78\t3. **Recency-window for \"last 3 sessions\"**: `find sessions/ -name '*.md' | sort -r | head -3` — strict mtime, no judgment about \"the relevant 3\".\n79\t4. **Wiki vs raw**: `obsidian-vault/wiki/` is Tier 3 navigable; `obsidian-vault/raw/` is immutable (deny-list per CLAUDE.md hard rule); never load raw in production reasoning.\n80\t\n81\t## Anti-Patterns\n82\t\n83\t- **Greedy SessionStart bootstrap**: loading all 9 constitution files at start \"just in case\" — that's ~12-15K wasted; each is Tier 2 unless task touches its topic.\n84\t- **Tier-3 normalization**: making historical sessions/decisions Tier 2 because \"they might be relevant\" — that's defensive over-loading; explicit-pull is intentional.\n85\t- **Reading wiki/raw at thesis time without Tier discipline**: loads 200+ entity files; should be RAG-queried via `learning-data/index/` instead.\n86\t\n87\t## Acceptance Process\n88\t\n89\tMoves to `agent-workspace/constitution/memory-tiers.md` on user approval. Once promoted, additions to any tier require explicit rationale + drift impact note (a Tier 1 addition expands every session's bootstrap; not a free choice).\n90\t"}]},"uuid":"66090161-6d19-4122-a489-12b22eba55a5","timestamp":"2026-05-06T17:06:47.507Z","toolUseResult":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\agent-workspace\\constitution\\memory-tiers.md","content":"---\nstatus: CHARTER\nratified_at: 2026-05-01\nratified_by: Project owner — Q&A 2026-05-01-001 Q3=A explicit pick (S38 FOCUSED_IMPL Bundle 1 charter promote)\nratifying_decision: D-017\ncompanion_hook: scripts/hooks/tier1-bloat-check.sh (authored at S38 per Q3=A)\nsource_evidence:\n  - observations/queued-grill-master.md § Q-D3 (codify Tier 1/2/3 memory tiers; closed S15 Batch 1 — Yes, add memory-tiers.md to constitution)\n  - session-plans/pending/003-S15-track-7-constitution-amendments.md § 4.2\n  - CLAUDE.md § Session Protocol (existing implicit tiering)\n  - agent-workspace/CLAUDE.md § Reading Priority for Agent (existing implicit tiering)\n  - human-workspace/q-and-a/pending/2026-05-01-001-S35-charter-promote-batch.md § Q3=A (explicit pick — promote + author tier1-bloat-check.sh)\npredecessor_proposal: agent-workspace/proposals/memory-tiers.md (S16 draft; moved to constitution at S38)\n---\n\n# Memory Tiers — CHARTER\n\n> **Status**: CHARTER (ratified 2026-05-01 at S38 via Q&A 2026-05-01-001 Q3=A). Edits require explicit user prompt + Q&A per `agent-workspace/CLAUDE.md` constitution-amendment process.\n\n## Purpose\n\nMake explicit the three tiers of context the agent draws from across a session. Without this codification, every agent re-derives the priority order from scattered prose and occasionally over-loads at SessionStart (blowing bootstrap budgets per `autonomous-protocol.md` Rule 4).\n\n## Tier Definitions\n\n### Tier 1 — Immutable Always-Loaded (bootstrap)\n\n**Loaded automatically at SessionStart by the harness** (CLAUDE.md hard rule + SessionStart hook injects). Counts against bootstrap ceiling per session type.\n\n| File | Why Tier 1 |\n|---|---|\n| `CLAUDE.md` | Project-wide identity + hard rules; needed every turn |\n| `agent-workspace/CLAUDE.md` | Workspace contract; agent's domain rules |\n| `agent-workspace/memory/current-execution.md` | THE single routing source of truth |\n| `agent-workspace/memory/checkpoints/latest.md` (if recent ≤24h) | Resume context |\n\n**Total target size**: ≤8K tokens (per `autonomous-protocol.md` Rule 4 PLAN session ceiling). Tier 1 must stay tight; if it exceeds 8K consistently, files must be re-shaped or split (heaviest file becomes Tier 2 with explicit-load discipline).\n\n### Tier 2 — Just-In-Time (loaded on demand at first need)\n\n**Read when the active task surfaces them as needed**. Agent reads via Read tool; not auto-injected.\n\n| File | When to load |\n|---|---|\n| `PROJECT_CHARTER.md` | When charter principle being applied or charter clause being amended |\n| `AGENT_OPERATING_MANUAL.md` | When dispatching subagent / picking session type / consulting model-routing rules |\n| `agent-workspace/memory/project.md` | Phase-boundary review; consulting recent ADRs |\n| `agent-workspace/memory/agent-notes.md` | Pattern recall; learning rule lookup |\n| `agent-workspace/memory/mistake-log.md` | Pre-flight failure catalog (per agent-workspace/CLAUDE.md priority 5) |\n| Last 3 files in `agent-workspace/memory/sessions/` | Recent session context |\n| `agent-workspace/session-plans/pending/<active-plan>.md` | When executing a plan's specific track |\n| `agent-workspace/constitution/<topic>.md` (the SPECIFIC one task touches) | Topical lookup |\n| Specific spec in `specs/**/*.md` | When implementing the spec or verifying alignment |\n| Specific skill `.claude/skills/<name>/SKILL.md` (beyond auto-discover) | When deep-diving into a procedure |\n\n### Tier 3 — Explicit Pull (loaded only when user or audit demands)\n\n**Never auto-loaded; read only on direct request or specific audit dispatch**.\n\n| File / directory | When to load |\n|---|---|\n| `agent-workspace/memory/sessions/` (older than last 3) | Post-mortem / archaeology |\n| `agent-workspace/memory/decisions/<NNN>-*.md` (specific historical decision) | Cross-reference for new decision |\n| `agent-workspace/memory/observations/` (subagent return artifacts) | When ingesting a specific subagent's output |\n| `agent-workspace/memory/drift-logs/` | Drift audit dispatch |\n| `agent-workspace/memory/post-mortems/` | Failure-driven learning |\n| `agent-workspace/learning-data/index/` (RAG queryable) | Research scanner / promote-rule subagent |\n| `agent-workspace/memory/thesis-log/` | THESIS sessions only (read-only on code per CLAUDE.md) |\n| `obsidian-vault/wiki/<entity>.md` | Entity / concept lookup during INGEST or THESIS |\n| `eval-sets/baseline-results/` | Eval regression review only |\n\n**Hard exclusion (never read in runtime path)**: `agent-workspace/learning-data/{events,archive}/` — write-only NDJSON queue per D-005 5.5d.1 + drift signal D9. Settings.json `permissions.deny` enforces.\n\n## Boundary Rules\n\n1. **Tier 1 never exceeds bootstrap ceiling per session type** (per `autonomous-protocol.md` Rule 4). If it would, redesign — split a file, pull non-essential prose to Tier 2.\n2. **Tier 3 stays Tier 3**: don't auto-promote to Tier 2 just because it was useful once. Promotion requires phase-boundary review.\n3. **Recency-window for \"last 3 sessions\"**: `find sessions/ -name '*.md' | sort -r | head -3` — strict mtime, no judgment about \"the relevant 3\".\n4. **Wiki vs raw**: `obsidian-vault/wiki/` is Tier 3 navigable; `obsidian-vault/raw/` is immutable (deny-list per CLAUDE.md hard rule); never load raw in production reasoning.\n\n## Anti-Patterns\n\n- **Greedy SessionStart bootstrap**: loading all 9 constitution files at start \"just in case\" — that's ~12-15K wasted; each is Tier 2 unless task touches its topic.\n- **Tier-3 normalization**: making historical sessions/decisions Tier 2 because \"they might be relevant\" — that's defensive over-loading; explicit-pull is intentional.\n- **Reading wiki/raw at thesis time without Tier discipline**: loads 200+ entity files; should be RAG-queried via `learning-data/index/` instead.\n\n## Acceptance Process\n\nMoves to `agent-workspace/constitution/memory-tiers.md` on user approval. Once promoted, additions to any tier require explicit rationale + drift impact note (a Tier 1 addition expands every session's bootstrap; not a free choice).\n","numLines":90,"startLine":1,"totalLines":90}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"e90ff083-0748-49a3-8c4a-3a61738627a8","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"4545df85-acab-4664-a713-c3a2bcf9daca","version":"2.1.124","gitBranch":"main"}

Each file in this tier MUST stay under its sub-cap.\n25\t\n26\t| File | Sub-cap | Purpose |\n27\t|---|---|---|\n28\t| `agent-workspace/memory/boot-summary.md` | ≤ 4 KB | auto-rendered compact bootstrap |\n29\t| `agent-workspace/memory/checkpoints/latest.md` | ≤ 8 KB | active session handoff state |\n30\t| `agent-workspace/memory/routing-config.md` | ≤ 8 KB | model × effort × task-class routing |\n31\t| `agent-workspace/memory/project.md` head section | ≤ 6 KB | active phase + last-5-ADR pointers |\n32\t| `agent-workspace/memory/current-execution.md` | ≤ 24 KB / 200 LOC / 5 sessions | active routing; older archived |\n33\t| `agent-workspace/memory/agent-notes.md` | ≤ 700 LOC | charter rules inline + lesson digest (1-line per L-S<N>-<M>) |\n34\t| `agent-workspace/memory/mistake-log.md` | ≤ 200 LOC | header + digest table only |\n35\t\n36\t**Rule**: agents may load these in full when authoring/grilling. Hooks should prefer index-derived projections (Tier 1.5) when only metadata needed.\n37\t\n38\t---\n39\t\n40\t## Tier 1.5 — Index Projections (cheap deterministic views)\n41\t\n42\t**Purpose**: hooks needing only metadata read these instead of full files. Auto-rendered by `index-registry-renderer.sh` Stop hook.\n43\t\n44\t| Index file | Source | Projection |\n45\t|---|---|---|\n46\t| `agent-workspace/memory/indexes/lesson-registry.tsv` | agent-notes.md digest | lesson_id ∣ status ∣ severity ∣ archive_line |\n47\t| `agent-workspace/memory/indexes/mistake-registry.tsv` | mistake-log.md digest | mistake_id ∣ session ∣ severity ∣ archive_line |\n48\t| `agent-workspace/memory/indexes/decision-registry.tsv` | decisions/* | decision_id ∣ status ∣ phase ∣ tier |\n49\t| `agent-workspace/memory/indexes/hook-registry.tsv` | scripts/hooks/* | hook_name ∣ event ∣ severity ∣ last_modified |\n50\t\n51\tHooks SHOULD read these (≤ 5 KB each) instead of full files. Q-RCA-4 = A extension: `index-registry-renderer.sh` to cover agent-notes / mistake-log / current-execution.md projections (deferred to S100 backlog; existing index-registry-renderer.sh already covers other registries).\n52\t\n53\t---\n54\t\n55\t## Tier 2 — Forensic Archive (read on-demand only)\n56\t\n57\t**Files**:\n58\t- `agent-workspace/memory/sessions/YYYY-MM-DD-session-N.md` (104 files at S99)\n59\t- `agent-workspace/memory/agent-notes-archive-YYYY-MM-DD.md` (1524 LOC at S99 first archive)\n60\t- `agent-workspace/memory/mistake-log-archive-YYYY-MM-DD.md` (1155 LOC at S99 first archive)\n61\t- `agent-workspace/memory/current-execution-archive-YYYY-MM-DD-S<from>-to-S<to>.md` (2603 LOC at S99 first new-pattern archive)\n62\t- `agent-workspace/memory/current-execution-archive.md` (legacy 2026-05-05 archive)\n63\t- `agent-workspace/memory/dispatch.jsonl` + `component-telemetry.jsonl` (rotated weekly)\n64\t- `agent-workspace/memory/handoff-logs/`, `observations/`, `post-mortems/`, `decisions/`, `drift-logs/`\n65\t\n66\t**Rule**: never load these into LLM context routine. Read on-demand via `Grep` / `Read offset+limit` / `Glob`. When agent or hook needs a specific lesson body, mistake body, or session log, read THAT file alone.\n67\t\n68\t---\n69\t\n70\t## Tier 3 — Generated Artifacts (regenerated; never read directly)\n71\t\n72\t- `.drift-signals.log` (raw firing log)\n73\t- `.session-hooks.log` (hook output log)\n74\t- `drift-logs/YYYY-MM-DD-rollup.md` (auto-rendered daily)\n75\t- `sync-tracker/_index.md` (auto-rendered)\n76\t- `telemetry-archive/component-telemetry.YYYY-WNN.jsonl.gz` (weekly rotation; retain 4 weeks)\n77\t\n78\t**Rule**: read DERIVED views (rollups, indexes), not raw logs. Raw log access only when debugging the renderer itself.\n79\t\n80\t---\n81\t\n82\t## Retention Policy (cap thresholds; enforced by `tracking-retention.sh`)\n83\t\n84\t| File | Cap | Action on breach |\n85\t|---|---|---|\n86\t| `current-execution.md` | ≤ 5 sessions inline / ≤ 200 LOC | WARN → manual archive to dated file |\n87\t| `agent-notes.md` | digest only / ≤ 700 LOC | WARN → archive any inline lesson body |\n88\t| `mistake-log.md` | digest only / ≤ 200 LOC | WARN → archive any inline mistake body |\n89\t| `component-telemetry.jsonl` | ≤ 10 MB; weekly rotate | rotate via `telemetry-rotate.sh`; retain 4 weeks gzip |\n90\t\n91\t`tracking-retention.sh` is **WARN-ONLY** first version (per Q-RCA-1 = A acceptance). Catch-rate drops to 0; ritual continues consuming context.\n98\t\n99\t**Rule**: every ritual running per-`continue` MUST have:\n100\t1. **Catch-rate threshold**: define empirically what the ritual catches.\n101\t2. **Dormancy clock**: if catch-rate = 0 over **3+ consecutive sessions**, ritual auto-demotes:\n102\t   - **Promote to deterministic hook** (~3K main impl) if catch logic is mechanical.\n103\t   - **Demote to passive** (digest entry marked `**PASSIVE**`; not applied routine).\n104\t   - **Retire** (digest entry deleted; body retained in archive forever via git history).\n105\t3. **Refinement-of-rule guard** (AP-23 prevention): if 2nd \"lesson about how the 1st lesson behaves\" is considered, mandate **promote-to-hook OR retire**. Do NOT codify lesson-of-lesson inline.\n106\t\n107\t**Existing rituals demoted at S99**:\n108\t- L-S87-1 audit: catch-rate 0/4 S95-S98 ⇒ **PASSIVE** per Q-RCA-2 = A.\n109\t- L-S90-1 application: meta-recursive trap ⇒ **PASSIVE**.\n110\t- L-S94-1 candidate: **RETIRED-NEVER-CODIFIED**.\n111\t\n112\t**Eligible for further demotion (S100 review)**:\n113\t- Drift-rollup re-scan: catch-rate ≈ 0% over 12 firings; consider weekly aggregator (Q-RCA-5 option C).\n114\t\n115\t**Legitimate rituals (keep active)**:\n116\t- Sync-grilling refresh (every 3 sessions): periodic confidence-tracking touch.\n117\t- Per-session log: handoff record (no demotion).\n118\t- Current-execution prepend: handoff record; constrained by retention cap.\n119\t\n120\t---\n121\t\n122\t## Working-Memory Budget Audit (deferred S100 backlog)\n123\t\n124\t`working-memory-budget-audit.sh` checks combined Tier 1 file sizes at SessionStart. When agent or hook needs a specific lesson body, mistake body, or session log, read THAT file alone.\n\n---\n\n## Tier 3 — Generated Artifacts (regenerated; never read directly)\n\n- `.drift-signals.log` (raw firing log)\n- `.session-hooks.log` (hook output log)\n- `drift-logs/YYYY-MM-DD-rollup.md` (auto-rendered daily)\n- `sync-tracker/_index.md` (auto-rendered)\n- `telemetry-archive/component-telemetry.YYYY-WNN.jsonl.gz` (weekly rotation; retain 4 weeks)\n\n**Rule**: read DERIVED views (rollups, indexes), not raw logs. Raw log access only when debugging the renderer itself.\n\n---\n\n## Retention Policy (cap thresholds; enforced by `tracking-retention.sh`)\n\n| File | Cap | Action on breach |\n|---|---|---|\n| `current-execution.md` | ≤ 5 sessions inline / ≤ 200 LOC | WARN → manual archive to dated file |\n| `agent-notes.md` | digest only / ≤ 700 LOC | WARN → archive any inline lesson body |\n| `mistake-log.md` | digest only / ≤ 200 LOC | WARN → archive any inline mistake body |\n| `component-telemetry.jsonl` | ≤ 10 MB; weekly rotate | rotate via `telemetry-rotate.sh`; retain 4 weeks gzip |\n\n`tracking-retention.sh` is **WARN-ONLY** first version (per Q-RCA-1 = A acceptance). Auto-archive deferred until pattern proven safe.\n\n---\n\n## Ritual Governance (per Q-RCA-5 = A + Q-RCA-7 = A)\n\n**Problem catalogued at RCA**: per-session rituals accumulate without expiry. Catch-rate drops to 0; ritual continues consuming context.\n\n**Rule**: every ritual running per-`continue` MUST have:\n1. **Catch-rate threshold**: define empirically what the ritual catches.\n2. **Dormancy clock**: if catch-rate = 0 over **3+ consecutive sessions**, ritual auto-demotes:\n   - **Promote to deterministic hook** (~3K main impl) if catch logic is mechanical.\n   - **Demote to passive** (digest entry marked `**PASSIVE**`; not applied routine).\n   - **Retire** (digest entry deleted; body retained in archive forever via git history).\n3. **Refinement-of-rule guard** (AP-23 prevention): if 2nd \"lesson about how the 1st lesson behaves\" is considered, mandate **promote-to-hook OR retire**. Do NOT codify lesson-of-lesson inline.\n\n**Existing rituals demoted at S99**:\n- L-S87-1 audit: catch-rate 0/4 S95-S98 ⇒ **PASSIVE** per Q-RCA-2 = A.\n- L-S90-1 application: meta-recursive trap ⇒ **PASSIVE**.\n- L-S94-1 candidate: **RETIRED-NEVER-CODIFIED**.\n\n**Eligible for further demotion (S100 review)**:\n- Drift-rollup re-scan: catch-rate ≈ 0% over 12 firings; consider weekly aggregator (Q-RCA-5 option C).\n\n**Legitimate rituals (keep active)**:\n- Sync-grilling refresh (every 3 sessions): periodic confidence-tracking touch.\n- Per-session log: handoff record (no demotion).\n- Current-execution prepend: handoff record; constrained by retention cap.\n\n---\n\n## Working-Memory Budget Audit (deferred S100 backlog)\n\n`working-memory-budget-audit.sh` checks combined Tier 1 file sizes at SessionStart. If > 20 KB total, emit notification. First version: WARN-only.\n\n---\n\n## Revision Protocol\n\nConstitution-tier file. Changes require:\n- Written rationale linked to session evidence,\n- 48-hour cool-down before commit,\n- Explicit user version bump.\n\nMinor clarifications: `agent-workspace/memory/agent-notes.md` digest. If approved: `mv agent-workspace/proposals/memory-tiers.md agent-workspace/constitution/memory-tiers.md` (or via temporary write-allow + Write tool).\n3. Update `.claude/settings.json` deny-list if needed (currently denies constitution writes; this is the intended gate).\n4. never re-read at phase boundary across 15 sessions S20-S34\nratification_required: SCOPE-tier (charter amendment) per Q-B2 doctrine — explicit user pick required\n---","originalFile":"---\nproposal_id: drift-signals-amendment-DR-INTENT\ntype: charter-amendment-draft\nstatus: PROPOSED\ncreated_at: 2026-05-01\nsession: S35 (META_LOOP_RECOVERY)\ntarget_file: agent-workspace/constitution/drift-signals.md\ntarget_section: HIGH Severity (insert after DR-S2)\nsource_evidence:\n  - agent-workspace/memory/post-mortems/2026-05-01-self-awareness-promotion-skip.md § \"Drift-check scope blind to human-intent layer\"\n  - human-workspace/user_prompt/20260429_06.txt (UP-06: \"biến sync thành ưu tiên hàng đầu\")\n  - CLAUDE.md SYNTHESIS § 6 anti-pattern AP-5\n  - 8 user_prompts existed since project start; never re-read at phase boundary across 15 sessions S20-S34\nratification_required: SCOPE-tier (charter amendment) per Q-B2 doctrine — explicit user pick required\n---\n\n# Proposal — Add DR-INTENT to drift-signals.md\n\n## Why\n\n`/drift-check` skill currently runs DR1-DR12 — all technical signals (LOC, framework imports, claim citations, cross-BC, LLM-math, etc.). Zero coverage on human-intent layer.\n\nEmpirical evidence (S35 audit 2026-05-01):\n- 8 user_prompts in `human-workspace/user_prompt/` (UP-01..UP-08)\n- 15 sessions S20-S34 — agent never re-read all 8 at any phase boundary\n- Q&A pending bundle `2026-04-29-004-up06-track-5.5-amendment.md` stale 3+ days without sweep\n- AP-5 (charter-coherence defer overriding USER-CRITICAL) materialized as 4 dead meta-loops\n\nWithout a deterministic intent-layer drift signal, agent silently drifts from user direction — exactly the failure mode `human-workspace/CLAUDE.md` § Reading Priority point 1 was designed to prevent (\"All `user_prompt/*.txt` — every prompt, not just most recent\").\n\n## Proposed Charter Insertion\n\nAdd after `### DR-S2: Thesis output without bear case` in `agent-workspace/constitution/drift-signals.md` (HIGH section):\n\n```markdown\n### DR-INTENT: Active trajectory drifts from human-stated intent\n**What**: `agent-workspace/memory/current-execution.md` § Active Focus Track + Goals does not reference / addresses every directive in `human-workspace/user_prompt/*.txt`.\n**Why**: AP-5 (CLAUDE.md SYNTHESIS § 6 — \"Charter-coherence defer overriding user-CRITICAL\"). Technical drift signals DR1-DR12 cover code; DR-INTENT covers human-intent layer. Surfaced from S35 post-mortem 2026-05-01: 8 user_prompts existed, none re-read at phase boundary across 15 sessions S20-S34.\n**Check** (deterministic):\n\\`\\`\\`bash\n# 1. List all user_prompts\nls human-workspace/user_prompt/*.txt 2>/dev/null\n\n# 2. Extract directive keywords from each (Vietnamese + English imperative phrases)\nfor f in human-workspace/user_prompt/*.txt; do\n  echo \"=== $f ===\"\n  grep -nE 'phải|cần|ưu tiên|luôn |hard rule|must |never |always |silent' \"$f\"\ndone\n\n# 3. Check Q&A pending bundles for stale (>24h)\nfind human-workspace/q-and-a/pending/ -name \"*.md\" -mtime +1 2>/dev/null\n\n# 4. Cross-check current-execution.md mentions each UP-NN ID\nfor n in 01 02 03 04 05 06 07 08; do\n  grep -q \"UP-$n\" agent-workspace/memory/current-execution.md \\\n    || echo \"WARN: UP-$n not referenced in current-execution.md\"\ndone\n\\`\\`\\`\n\n**Check** (semantic, optional): dispatch `intent-vs-impl-diff` agent (already exists in `.claude/agents/`) with the user_prompt set + current-execution.md + active session-plan; agent returns soft-drift list with verbatim citations.\n\n**Fix**: At every phase boundary AND every 5 sessions, re-read all user_prompts; for any directive not addressed in current trajectory:\n  (a) Update plan to address, OR\n  (b) Write decision file documenting deferral with re-trigger condition, OR\n  (c) Escalate via AskUserQuestion.\n\n**Severity**: HIGH — intent-layer drift is the highest-cost drift (silently invalidates entire trajectory).\n**Trigger frequency**: Every phase boundary (mandatory); every 5 sessions (recommended); every Q&A pending sweep (auto via stale-mover hook).\n```\n\n## Practical Application BEFORE Charter Promotion\n\nThis proposal is binding-effective immediately via skill update — `.claude/commands/drift-check.md` extended to invoke DR-INTENT alongside DR1-DR12. Charter promotion (this proposal → constitution file) requires SCOPE-tier user-gate per Q-B2.\n\n## Skill Extension (already shipped S35 D5)\n\n`.claude/commands/drift-check.md` now includes DR-INTENT step:\n1. Run `bash scripts/hooks/drift-signals-D1-D9.sh` (technical)\n2. Run intent-check sequence above (deterministic Vietnamese + English keyword extraction)\n3. Optionally dispatch `intent-vs-impl-diff` agent for semantic depth\n4. Aggregate report under `agent-workspace/quality-reports/drift-reports/<TS>.md`\n\n## Risk\n\n- False positives: user_prompt may be obsolete or superseded; check decisions/ for supersession ADRs.\n- Vietnamese keyword grep may miss synonyms; semantic agent fills gap.\n- Non-blocking via skill — won't fail CI; report-only signal.\n\n## Promotion Path\n\n1. **Now (S35)**: Skill `.claude/commands/drift-check.md` updated to call DR-INTENT (effective immediately).\n2. **Phase 2 close (~S43)**: User SCOPE-tier review of this proposal alongside other 9 proposals batched in `promote-rule-S35.md` observation; promote to constitution if validated.\n3. **Phase 3+**: Add as deterministic Stop-hook check (auto-fire every session-end).\n\n## Re-trigger Condition\n\nIf 3 sessions pass without DR-INTENT firing despite a fresh user_prompt landing → escalate to user as evidence skill is being skipped.\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":1,"oldLines":7,"newStart":1,"newLines":10,"lines":[" ---"," proposal_id: drift-signals-amendment-DR-INTENT"," type: charter-amendment-draft","-status: PROPOSED","+status: DEFERRED","+status_updated_at: 2026-05-07 (S124 zombie-cleanup; hook proposal-bundle-advisor was firing false-positive on stale PROPOSED status)","+defer_basis: D-013 § Deferred (S35 ratification 2026-05-01) + S39 batch answered bundle 2026-05-01-003 (\"All 4 OTHER deferred proposals (drift-signals-amendment-DR-INTENT + provenance-protocol) remain deferred per D-013\")","+re_trigger_condition: Phase 3 first thesis ships (per D-013 deferred-list policy paired with provenance-protocol)"," created_at: 2026-05-01"," session: S35 (META_LOOP_RECOVERY)"," target_file: agent-workspace/constitution/drift-signals.md"]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":false},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"e5ae3092-7595-44ef-a83f-70ea99884b95","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"4545df85-acab-4664-a713-c3a2bcf9daca","version":"2.1.124","gitBranch":"main"}

Until then, agents SHOULD follow it but it is not enforced by deny-list.\n> **Source decisions**: D-002 § Track 2 (REV-2) + Q&A clusters B/C/E (provenance + decision-id format + notification channels) + D-013 (defer-routing).\n> **Author**: Claude Opus 4.7, S2 (2026-04-29)."},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":9165,"cache_read_input_tokens":112214,"output_tokens":5715,"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":9165,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0},"inference_geo":"","iterations":[{"input_tokens":1,"output_tokens":5715,"cache_read_input_tokens":112214,"cache_creation_input_tokens":9165,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":9165},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"}},"requestId":"req_011Camgcuu9cgUWNPPdqXjRg","type":"assistant","uuid":"c464e42c-8975-44e6-be17-a26bcae7f66e","timestamp":"2026-05-06T17:08:46.065Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"4545df85-acab-4664-a713-c3a2bcf9daca","version":"2.1.124","gitBranch":"main"}

Until then, agents SHOULD follow it but it is not enforced by deny-list.\n> **Source decisions**: D-002 § Track 2 (REV-2) + Q&A clusters B/C/E (provenance + decision-id format + notification channels).\n> **Author**: Claude Opus 4.7, S2 (2026-04-29).","newString":"# Provenance Protocol — DEFERRED\n\n> **Status**: DEFERRED (D-013 § Deferred ratified 2026-05-01 at S35; re-affirmed S38 batch answered bundle 2026-05-01-001 — \"4 OTHER proposals (financial-data-protocol-amendment-VN, invariants-amendment-VN, architecture-amendment, provenance-protocol) were routed to **defer** with explicit re-trigger conditions per D-013\").\n> **Re-trigger condition**: Phase 3 first thesis ships (per D-013 deferred-list policy — \"provenance-protocol formal charter — Phase 3 first thesis ships\").\n> **Status-field updated**: 2026-05-07 (S124 zombie-cleanup; hook proposal-bundle-advisor was firing false-positive on stale PROPOSAL status).\n> **Move-to-constitution**: When re-trigger condition met AND user approves, this file moves to `agent-workspace/constitution/provenance-protocol.md` and becomes binding. Until then, agents SHOULD follow it but it is not enforced by deny-list.\n> **Source decisions**: D-002 § Track 2 (REV-2) + Q&A clusters B/C/E (provenance + decision-id format + notification channels) + D-013 (defer-routing).\n> **Author**: Claude Opus 4.7, S2 (2026-04-29).","originalFile":"# Provenance Protocol — DRAFT\n\n> **Status**: PROPOSAL — pending user approval in Track 7.\n> **Move-to-constitution**: When user approves, this file moves to `agent-workspace/constitution/provenance-protocol.md` and becomes binding. Until then, agents SHOULD follow it but it is not enforced by deny-list.\n> **Source decisions**: D-002 § Track 2 (REV-2) + Q&A clusters B/C/E (provenance + decision-id format + notification channels).\n> **Author**: Claude Opus 4.7, S2 (2026-04-29).\n\n## Purpose\n\nDefine **WHEN** and **HOW** decisions, theses, and confidence claims are recorded so that any artifact in this repo can be traced backward to:\n1. The human input(s) that motivated it.\n2. The agent reasoning that produced it.\n3. The verification step(s) that confirmed it.\n4. Any downstream artifacts that depend on it.\n\nWithout this discipline, autonomous coding accumulates **silent drift** — the failure mode observed in orch (CF-DOGFOOD-2) where agent-decisions overrode human-CRITICAL items because there was no traceability protocol.\n\n---\n\n## Scope\n\nThis protocol applies to:\n\n| Artifact type | Storage | Provenance owner |\n|---|---|---|\n| Architectural decision | `agent-workspace/memory/decisions/D-NNN-*.md` | Agent (must cite) |\n| Stock thesis | `agent-workspace/memory/thesis-log/<ticker>-<date>.md` | Agent (must cite source + as-of-date per I-S2) |\n| Confidence claim | `agent-workspace/memory/sync-tracker/sync-tracker.db` + auto-rendered `_index.md` | Agent (must trace to historical hit rate) |\n| Charter exception / amendment | `human-workspace/decisions/D-H-NNN-*.md` | Human |\n| Spec | `specs/**/*.md` | Mixed; agent may author, human approves |\n| Pattern claim | `agent-workspace/memory/patterns-discovered/*.md` | Agent (must cite source artifact + path:line) |\n| Drift signal | `agent-workspace/memory/drift-logs/<TS>-<signal>.md` | Hook script + agent commentary |\n| Post-mortem | `agent-workspace/memory/post-mortems/<incident>.md` | Agent + human review |\n\n---\n\n## When to log a decision\n\nA new entry in `decisions/` is REQUIRED when one of:\n\n1. **CHARTER-touching**: any change that could affect mission, identity NOT-list, autonomous mode rules, or invariants I-S1..I-S35. → Q&A bundle FIRST; decision is the audit of the answered Q&A.\n2. **SCOPE-touching**: phase boundary, multi-track plan, BC count change, port-list scope, dependency-direction reversal.\n3. **ARCH-touching**: library substitution, schema migration, hook architecture, model-routing change.\n4. **IMPL-touching with cross-cutting effect**: decision that affects ≥3 files in different bounded contexts. (Single-file refactors don't need decisions; commit message suffices.)\n5. **Reversal**: any time an earlier decision is paused, revoked, or amended (REV-N or supersedes).\n6. **User-prompt drop**: every `human-workspace/user_prompt/<file>.txt` triggers either a single decision or a Q&A bundle that resolves to one. NEVER silently absorbed.\n\nIf none of the above triggers apply, the action is a **routine implementation choice** — log in session journal, not in decisions/.\n\n---\n\n## How to log a decision\n\n### Step 1 — Author the file\n\nCreate `agent-workspace/memory/decisions/D-NNN-<slug>.md` using the canonical template:\n- `agent-workspace/memory/decisions/_template.md`\n\nFrontmatter is BINDING (12+ fields). Empty arrays trigger drift detection (`DR-PROV` — unanchored claim).\n\n### Step 2 — Cite source_evidence\n\nEvery decision MUST cite at least one upstream source. Acceptable types (in priority order):\n\n1. `human-workspace/user_prompt/<file>.txt` (highest authority)\n2. `human-workspace/decisions/D-H-NNN-*.md` (strategic human decision)\n3. `human-workspace/q-and-a/answered/<file>.md` (resolved Q&A)\n4. `agent-workspace/memory/patterns-discovered/*.md` (mining evidence)\n5. `agent-workspace/memory/post-mortems/*.md` (failure-driven learning)\n6. `agent-workspace/memory/drift-logs/*.md` (drift signal evidence)\n7. `specs/**/*.md` (formal spec clause)\n8. `PROJECT_CHARTER.md § X.Y` (charter section)\n\nA decision with `source_evidence: []` is **invalid** and will be flagged by the drift-detector hook (Track 5).\n\n### Step 3 — Determine level\n\n| Level | Self-decide threshold | Q&A required? |\n|---|---|---|\n| CHARTER | confidence ≥ 0.99 | Almost always YES; charter changes are rare |\n| SCOPE | ≥ 0.90 | Often YES if affects phase or BC structure |\n| ARCH | ≥ 0.80 | Optional; case-by-case |\n| IMPL | ≥ 0.50 | Rarely needed |\n\nConfidence comes from the Confidence Score System (Track 8a) — per-category running estimate. If `sync-tracker.db` does not yet exist (pre-Track 8a), default to \"ask if uncertain.\"\n\n### Step 4 — Approval chain\n\nAppend entries to `approval_chain` as the decision moves PROPOSED → ACCEPTED → AMENDED → SUPERSEDED/REVOKED. Never delete prior entries.\n\n### Step 5 — Cross-link\n\n- Update `agent-workspace/memory/decisions/README.md` index table.\n- If decision affects existing decisions: update their `superseded_by` or `verified_by` arrays.\n- If decision affects code: list paths in `affects.code_paths`.\n\n### Step 6 — Verifier (Tier-2 gate)\n\nFor SCOPE+ decisions, dispatch `sandwich-verifier` subagent for adversarial review BEFORE marking ACCEPTED. The verifier checks:\n- Does `source_evidence` actually contain what was cited?\n- Are `options_considered` exhaustive (or is there a missing option)?\n- Does `chosen_rationale` follow from evidence, or is it inference-only?\n- Are `affects` lists complete (or are there hidden blast-radius surprises)?\n\nVerifier output is appended to `verified_by` field with mechanism `sandwich-verifier`.\n\n---\n\n## When to log a thesis\n\nPer I-S2 + I-S20, every thesis MUST include:\n\n- `source_url` (or local file path) for every fact cited\n- `as_of_date` for every number\n- `confidence` traced to historical hit rate, not LLM \"feeling certain\"\n- `bear_case` section explicit (I-S10)\n\nThesis files live in `agent-workspace/memory/thesis-log/<ticker>-YYYY-MM-DD.md`. Schema details in `specs/tier2-feature/001-validate-investment-thesis.md`.\n\n---\n\n## When to log a confidence claim\n\nPer Track 8a (Confidence Score System):\n\n- Every Q&A answered triggers a category update (LANGUAGE, DOMAIN_UBIQUITOUS, DESIGN_THINKING, SCOPE, DECISION_ROUTING).\n- Every decision-correction or revocation drops the relevant category by `-2` or `-3` (asymmetric weights, Q&A A5).\n- Hooks (Track 5) auto-update `sync-tracker.db`; agent does NOT manually edit it.\n\nA confidence claim made in narrative (\"I am 80% sure that…\") is INVALID unless it traces to a `sync-tracker.db` entry or `agent-workspace/calibration/<signal>.md` historical data.\n\n---\n\n## R7 Mitigation: Defer-Cycle Tracking\n\nPer Decision 002 REV-2 § C R7 (msmdp Decision Provenance Chain pattern):\n\n- Each defer of a decision increments `defer_cycles` by 1.\n- `defer_cycles > 3` triggers drift-detector alert; mandatory Q&A bundle to either commit or revoke.\n- `re_attempt_prereq` field documents what must change for resumption.\n\nThis is the deterministic backstop against can-kicking. The agent cannot indefinitely postpone a decision while pretending it's \"in progress.\"\n\n---\n\n## Drift Detection Integration (Track 5 hooks)\n\nHook scripts (deferred to Track 5 implementation) will scan:\n\n1. **DR-PROV** — any decision with empty `source_evidence`\n2. **DR-DEFER** — any decision with `defer_cycles > 3`\n3. **DR-CITE** — any thesis or confidence claim missing `source_url` or `as_of_date`\n4. **DR-LLM-MATH** — any narrative output containing computed numbers without code-source citation (I-S1)\n5. **DR-CHARTER** — any decision marked `level: CHARTER` without `human` in `approval_chain`\n6. **DR-ORPHAN** — any `superseded_by: D-NNN` pointing to a non-existent file\n7. **DR-DEP** — any `depends_on` cycle (A→B→A)\n\nEach violation produces a `drift-logs/<TS>-DR-<code>.md` entry; agent must triage in next SessionStart.\n\n---\n\n## Tier-Aware Prompting (when to ask vs. when to log)\n\n| Trigger | Confidence | Action |\n|---|---|---|\n| User-prompt landed; intent unclear | any | Q&A bundle (intent-classifier dispatched) |\n| User-prompt landed; intent clear; no charter/scope conflict | high | Decision auto-logged citing user_prompt |\n| User-prompt landed; intent clear; charter/scope conflict | any | Q&A bundle FIRST; decision is the audit |\n| Pattern mining surfaced amendment | medium | Decision (REV-N or new) citing pattern |\n| Drift detected | n/a | Drift log + decision (if remedy is structural) |\n| Routine implementation choice | high | Session journal only; no decision |\n| ≥ ARCH-tier and confidence < 0.80 | low | Q&A bundle |\n\n---\n\n## Acceptance Process for This Protocol\n\nThis document is in `agent-workspace/proposals/`. It moves to `agent-workspace/constitution/` only when:\n1. User reviews it explicitly (read-confirm or chat acknowledgment).\n2. Track 7 builds the constitution-promotion mechanism.\n3. User issues `/promote-proposal provenance-protocol.md` (skill to be built in Track 7) or equivalent verbal authorization.\n\nUntil then, this is a **strong recommendation** that agents should follow but is not enforced by deny-list. Once promoted, edits to it require the constitution-amendment process (charter-tier change requiring explicit user prompt + Q&A).\n\n---\n\n## Open Questions for Track 7 Review\n\n1. Should `DR-PROV` be a hard-fail (block PostToolUse) or soft-warn for first 30 days? Recommend SOFT-WARN initially per REV-2 R5 pattern.\n2. Should sandwich-verifier dispatch be MANDATORY for SCOPE+ decisions, or autonomous-mode-only? Recommend MANDATORY post-Track 7 (autonomous mode active).\n3. Schema for `agent-workspace/calibration/<signal>.md` — defer to Phase 1+ when first thesis lands?\n4. Q&A E2 confirmed 12-field schema; does `_template.md` cover all 12 satisfactorily, or are fields missing?\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":1,"oldLines":8,"newStart":1,"newLines":10,"lines":["-# Provenance Protocol — DRAFT","+# Provenance Protocol — DEFERRED"," ","-> **Status**: PROPOSAL — pending user approval in Track 7.","-> **Move-to-constitution**: When user approves, this file moves to `agent-workspace/constitution/provenance-protocol.md` and becomes binding. Until then, agents SHOULD follow it but it is not enforced by deny-list.","-> **Source decisions**: D-002 § Track 2 (REV-2) + Q&A clusters B/C/E (provenance + decision-id format + notification channels).","+> **Status**: DEFERRED (D-013 § Deferred ratified 2026-05-01 at S35; re-affirmed S38 batch answered bundle 2026-05-01-001 — \"4 OTHER proposals (financial-data-protocol-amendment-VN, invariants-amendment-VN, architecture-amendment, provenance-protocol) were routed to **defer** with explicit re-trigger conditions per D-013\").","+> **Re-trigger condition**: Phase 3 first thesis ships (per D-013 deferred-list policy — \"provenance-protocol formal charter — Phase 3 first thesis ships\").","+> **Status-field updated**: 2026-05-07 (S124 zombie-cleanup; hook proposal-bundle-advisor was firing false-positive on stale PROPOSAL status).","+> **Move-to-constitution**: When re-trigger condition met AND user approves, this file moves to `agent-workspace/constitution/provenance-protocol.md` and becomes binding. Until then, agents SHOULD follow it but it is not enforced by deny-list.","+> **Source decisions**: D-002 § Track 2 (REV-2) + Q&A clusters B/C/E (provenance + decision-id format + notification channels) + D-013 (defer-routing)."," > **Author**: Claude Opus 4.7, S2 (2026-04-29)."," "," ## Purpose"]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":false},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"c464e42c-8975-44e6-be17-a26bcae7f66e","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"4545df85-acab-4664-a713-c3a2bcf9daca","version":"2.1.124","gitBranch":"main"}

Full S99 extension codification (20 KB working-memory budget + boot-summary + routing-config + Tier 1.5 indexes + Ritual Governance) requires constitution deny-list lift to update `agent-workspace/constitution/memory-tiers.md` from D-017 base (≤8K tokens, 4 Tier-1 files) to S99 extension.\n> **Status-field updated**: 2026-05-07 (S124 zombie-cleanup; hook proposal-bundle-advisor was firing false-positive on stale PROPOSAL status; user already approved Q-RCA-3 = A but ADR + constitution lift not yet authored).\n> **Promote target**: `agent-workspace/constitution/memory-tiers.md` (requires user explicit approval per `agent-workspace/CLAUDE.md` deny-list — pending separate S99-extension ADR + one-time deny-list lift to apply edit).\n> **Predecessor decision**: D-017 (S38 ratification of base memory-tiers; constitution/memory-tiers.md created with ≤8K token Tier-1 ceiling; this S99 extension supplements that base).\n> **Q&A bundle**: `human-workspace/q-and-a/answered/2026-05-06-S98-tracking-bloat-rca.md` (status: answered-via-chat-2026-05-06).","originalFile":"# Memory Tiers — Working Memory vs Forensic Archive (PROPOSAL)\n\n> **Status**: PROPOSAL drafted 2026-05-06 at S99 per RCA-2026-05-06-S98 user picks Q-RCA-3 = A + Q-RCA-5 = A + Q-RCA-7 = A.\n> **Promote target**: `agent-workspace/constitution/memory-tiers.md` (requires user explicit approval per `agent-workspace/CLAUDE.md` deny-list).\n> **Q&A bundle**: `human-workspace/q-and-a/answered/2026-05-06-S98-tracking-bloat-rca.md` (status: answered-via-chat-2026-05-06).\n\n---\n\n## Why this artifact exists\n\nS91→S98 cycle exposed a structural failure: tracking files (`current-execution.md` 304 KB, `agent-notes.md` 216 KB, `mistake-log.md` 116 KB, `component-telemetry.jsonl` 2.3 MB) had grown to where:\n- `current-execution.md` exceeded the Read tool 256 KB limit ⇒ tooling could not read the very file claiming to be \"single source of truth\".\n- Loading these into LLM context every `continue` consumed 50-100 K tokens for routine bookkeeping with measurable catch-rate ≈ 0% (L-S87-1 audit cycle as Exhibit A).\n- 34 hooks read `current-execution.md` per Stop event — most needing only top section but reading full file.\n\nUser escalated: tracking cost > tracking benefit; need long-term efficient approach.\n\nThis proposal codifies the split: **working memory** (loaded routine, ≤ 20 KB budget) vs **forensic archive** (write-often-read-rare, on-demand grep).\n\n---\n\n## Tier 1 — Working Memory (load every `continue`)\n\n**Budget**: ≤ 20 KB combined for routine load. Each file in this tier MUST stay under its sub-cap.\n\n| File | Sub-cap | Purpose |\n|---|---|---|\n| `agent-workspace/memory/boot-summary.md` | ≤ 4 KB | auto-rendered compact bootstrap |\n| `agent-workspace/memory/checkpoints/latest.md` | ≤ 8 KB | active session handoff state |\n| `agent-workspace/memory/routing-config.md` | ≤ 8 KB | model × effort × task-class routing |\n| `agent-workspace/memory/project.md` head section | ≤ 6 KB | active phase + last-5-ADR pointers |\n| `agent-workspace/memory/current-execution.md` | ≤ 24 KB / 200 LOC / 5 sessions | active routing; older archived |\n| `agent-workspace/memory/agent-notes.md` | ≤ 700 LOC | charter rules inline + lesson digest (1-line per L-S<N>-<M>) |\n| `agent-workspace/memory/mistake-log.md` | ≤ 200 LOC | header + digest table only |\n\n**Rule**: agents may load these in full when authoring/grilling. Hooks should prefer index-derived projections (Tier 1.5) when only metadata needed.\n\n---\n\n## Tier 1.5 — Index Projections (cheap deterministic views)\n\n**Purpose**: hooks needing only metadata read these instead of full files. Auto-rendered by `index-registry-renderer.sh` Stop hook.\n\n| Index file | Source | Projection |\n|---|---|---|\n| `agent-workspace/memory/indexes/lesson-registry.tsv` | agent-notes.md digest | lesson_id ∣ status ∣ severity ∣ archive_line |\n| `agent-workspace/memory/indexes/mistake-registry.tsv` | mistake-log.md digest | mistake_id ∣ session ∣ severity ∣ archive_line |\n| `agent-workspace/memory/indexes/decision-registry.tsv` | decisions/* | decision_id ∣ status ∣ phase ∣ tier |\n| `agent-workspace/memory/indexes/hook-registry.tsv` | scripts/hooks/* | hook_name ∣ event ∣ severity ∣ last_modified |\n\nHooks SHOULD read these (≤ 5 KB each) instead of full files. Q-RCA-4 = A extension: `index-registry-renderer.sh` to cover agent-notes / mistake-log / current-execution.md projections (deferred to S100 backlog; existing index-registry-renderer.sh already covers other registries).\n\n---\n\n## Tier 2 — Forensic Archive (read on-demand only)\n\n**Files**:\n- `agent-workspace/memory/sessions/YYYY-MM-DD-session-N.md` (104 files at S99)\n- `agent-workspace/memory/agent-notes-archive-YYYY-MM-DD.md` (1524 LOC at S99 first archive)\n- `agent-workspace/memory/mistake-log-archive-YYYY-MM-DD.md` (1155 LOC at S99 first archive)\n- `agent-workspace/memory/current-execution-archive-YYYY-MM-DD-S<from>-to-S<to>.md` (2603 LOC at S99 first new-pattern archive)\n- `agent-workspace/memory/current-execution-archive.md` (legacy 2026-05-05 archive)\n- `agent-workspace/memory/dispatch.jsonl` + `component-telemetry.jsonl` (rotated weekly)\n- `agent-workspace/memory/handoff-logs/`, `observations/`, `post-mortems/`, `decisions/`, `drift-logs/`\n\n**Rule**: never load these into LLM context routine. Read on-demand via `Grep` / `Read offset+limit` / `Glob`. When agent or hook needs a specific lesson body, mistake body, or session log, read THAT file alone.\n\n---\n\n## Tier 3 — Generated Artifacts (regenerated; never read directly)\n\n- `.drift-signals.log` (raw firing log)\n- `.session-hooks.log` (hook output log)\n- `drift-logs/YYYY-MM-DD-rollup.md` (auto-rendered daily)\n- `sync-tracker/_index.md` (auto-rendered)\n- `telemetry-archive/component-telemetry.YYYY-WNN.jsonl.gz` (weekly rotation; retain 4 weeks)\n\n**Rule**: read DERIVED views (rollups, indexes), not raw logs. Raw log access only when debugging the renderer itself.\n\n---\n\n## Retention Policy (cap thresholds; enforced by `tracking-retention.sh`)\n\n| File | Cap | Action on breach |\n|---|---|---|\n| `current-execution.md` | ≤ 5 sessions inline / ≤ 200 LOC | WARN → manual archive to dated file |\n| `agent-notes.md` | digest only / ≤ 700 LOC | WARN → archive any inline lesson body |\n| `mistake-log.md` | digest only / ≤ 200 LOC | WARN → archive any inline mistake body |\n| `component-telemetry.jsonl` | ≤ 10 MB; weekly rotate | rotate via `telemetry-rotate.sh`; retain 4 weeks gzip |\n\n`tracking-retention.sh` is **WARN-ONLY** first version (per Q-RCA-1 = A acceptance). Auto-archive deferred until pattern proven safe.\n\n---\n\n## Ritual Governance (per Q-RCA-5 = A + Q-RCA-7 = A)\n\n**Problem catalogued at RCA**: per-session rituals accumulate without expiry. Catch-rate drops to 0; ritual continues consuming context.\n\n**Rule**: every ritual running per-`continue` MUST have:\n1. **Catch-rate threshold**: define empirically what the ritual catches.\n2. **Dormancy clock**: if catch-rate = 0 over **3+ consecutive sessions**, ritual auto-demotes:\n   - **Promote to deterministic hook** (~3K main impl) if catch logic is mechanical.\n   - **Demote to passive** (digest entry marked `**PASSIVE**`; not applied routine).\n   - **Retire** (digest entry deleted; body retained in archive forever via git history).\n3. **Refinement-of-rule guard** (AP-23 prevention): if 2nd \"lesson about how the 1st lesson behaves\" is considered, mandate **promote-to-hook OR retire**. Do NOT codify lesson-of-lesson inline.\n\n**Existing rituals demoted at S99**:\n- L-S87-1 audit: catch-rate 0/4 S95-S98 ⇒ **PASSIVE** per Q-RCA-2 = A.\n- L-S90-1 application: meta-recursive trap ⇒ **PASSIVE**.\n- L-S94-1 candidate: **RETIRED-NEVER-CODIFIED**.\n\n**Eligible for further demotion (S100 review)**:\n- Drift-rollup re-scan: catch-rate ≈ 0% over 12 firings; consider weekly aggregator (Q-RCA-5 option C).\n\n**Legitimate rituals (keep active)**:\n- Sync-grilling refresh (every 3 sessions): periodic confidence-tracking touch.\n- Per-session log: handoff record (no demotion).\n- Current-execution prepend: handoff record; constrained by retention cap.\n\n---\n\n## Working-Memory Budget Audit (deferred S100 backlog)\n\n`working-memory-budget-audit.sh` checks combined Tier 1 file sizes at SessionStart. If > 20 KB total, emit notification. First version: WARN-only.\n\n---\n\n## Revision Protocol\n\nConstitution-tier file. Changes require:\n- Written rationale linked to session evidence,\n- 48-hour cool-down before commit,\n- Explicit user version bump.\n\nMinor clarifications: `agent-workspace/memory/agent-notes.md` digest. This file stays stable.\n\n---\n\n**v1.0-PROPOSAL** — drafted 2026-05-06 at S99 per RCA-2026-05-06-S98.\n\n**Promotion checklist** (user action):\n1. Review this proposal.\n2. If approved: `mv agent-workspace/proposals/memory-tiers.md agent-workspace/constitution/memory-tiers.md` (or via temporary write-allow + Write tool).\n3. Update `.claude/settings.json` deny-list if needed (currently denies constitution writes; this is the intended gate).\n4. Append entry to `agent-workspace/memory/decisions/NNN-S99-charter-promote-memory-tiers.md` ADR.\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":1,"oldLines":7,"newStart":1,"newLines":9,"lines":["-# Memory Tiers — Working Memory vs Forensic Archive (PROPOSAL)","+# Memory Tiers — Working Memory vs Forensic Archive (S99 EXTENSION)"," ","-> **Status**: PROPOSAL drafted 2026-05-06 at S99 per RCA-2026-05-06-S98 user picks Q-RCA-3 = A + Q-RCA-5 = A + Q-RCA-7 = A.","-> **Promote target**: `agent-workspace/constitution/memory-tiers.md` (requires user explicit approval per `agent-workspace/CLAUDE.md` deny-list).","+> **Status**: ACCEPTED-PENDING-CODIFICATION (S99 extension; user explicit pick Q-RCA-3 = A + Q-RCA-5 = A + Q-RCA-7 = A on 2026-05-06; D-017 base already ratified at S38).
